# Every winning phrase, scored on every model

Each table is one question. Each row is the Strict Highest leader from one edition. **Goal** is the yes/no target, or the named option on a choice question. Scores are that phrase’s p(goal) or p(option). Luna is blank when she refused. Decider is blank when we had no sample.
